    拼写纠错模型

    拼写纠错

    在电商系统中，抽取商品属性（如品牌、型号、颜色、尺寸、材质等）是实现商品标准化、搜索匹配、推荐引擎、知识图谱构建等关键任务的重要步骤。而在此之前进行拼写纠错，是确保属性抽取准确性和稳定性的一个不可或缺的预处理环节。

    拼写纠错的作用

    纠正用户或商家输入的错误拼写

    商品标题、描述或属性字段中，经常存在拼写错误，如：

    “三星”写成“三生”

    “Nike”写成“Nkie”

    “防水”写成“防谁”

    拼写纠错可以通过语言模型或词典比对将这些错误纠正为正确形式，提升后续处理准确率。

    提高商品属性抽取的准确性

    属性抽取依赖关键字或上下文识别错误拼写会导致抽取失败。如模型无法识别“棉麻职料”（应为“棉麻织料”）的材质属性。拼写纠错后，抽取模块才能正常识别关键词、上下文。

    减少冗余属性值

    拼写错误可能导致同一属性值被误识别为多个，如品牌：“adidas”、“adidass”、“adids” 会被误认为是不同品牌，造成数据混乱。拼写标准化后，统一归并属性值，便于商品聚合和统计。

    提升用户搜索与推荐的准确性

    用户搜索“iphone 手机壳”可能拼错为“iphnoe 手机可”，拼写纠错可以矫正后再进行搜索匹配。推荐系统基于商品属性分析，错误拼写会降低推荐质量。

    常用的拼写纠错方法

    基于词典匹配（如品牌词典、材质词典）。

    编辑距离算法（如 Levenshtein Distance）。

    基于语言模型的上下文拼写纠错。

    拼音相似匹配（针对中文拼写）。

    这里我们采用基于模型的方法进行拼写纠错。

    模型定义

    src\models_def\spell_check.py：

    import torch

    import torch.nn as nn

    from transformers import AutoTokenizer, BertModel

    class SpellCheckModel(nn.Module):

    """拼写检查模型"""

    def __init__(self, model_name: str):

    super().__init__()

    self.tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(model_name)

    self.model = BertModel.from_pretrained(model_name)

    初始化 lm_head 并共享嵌入层权重

    hidden_size = self.model.config.hidden_size

    vocab_size = self.model.config.vocab_size

    self.lm_head = nn.Linear(hidden_size, vocab_size)

    self.lm_head.weight = self.model.embeddings.word_embeddings.weight

    self.loss_fn = nn.CrossEntropyLoss()

    def forward(self, input_ids, attention_mask=None, labels=None):

    output = self.model(input_ids, attention_mask)

    logits = self.lm_head(output.last_hidden_state)

    loss = 0.0

    if labels is not None:

    loss = self.loss_fn(logits.view(-1, logits.size(-1)), labels.view(-1))

    return {"loss": loss, "logits": logits}

    @torch.inference_mode()

    def predict(self, text: str | list[str], device=torch.device("cpu"), batch_size=8):

    self.eval()

    self.to(device)

    res: list[str] = []

    统一转换为列表

    input_texts = text if isinstance(text, list) else [text]

    逐批次处理

    for i in range(0, len(input_texts), batch_size):

    batch_texts = input_texts[i : i + batch_size]

    inputs = self.tokenizer(

    batch_texts,

    max_length=256,

    truncation=True,

    padding=True,

    return_tensors="pt",

    ).to(device)

    outputs = self.forward(inputs["input_ids"], inputs["attention_mask"])

    pred_ids = torch.argmax(outputs["logits"], dim=-1)

    将 attention_mask 为 0 的位置替换为 pad_token_id

    pred_ids[inputs["attention_mask"] == 0] = self.tokenizer.pad_token_id

    批量解码

    batch_res = self.tokenizer.batch_decode(

    pred_ids, skip_special_tokens=True, clean_up_tokenization_spaces=True

    )

    去除空格

    batch_res = [i.replace(" ", "") for i in batch_res]

    res.extend(batch_res)

    return res if isinstance(text, list) else res[0]

    数据预处理

    src\preprocess\process.py：

    import os

    import random

    from datasets import Dataset

    from torch.utils.data import Subset

    from datasets import load_from_disk

    from torch.utils.data import DataLoader

    class Processor:

    def __init__(

    self,

    data_path,

    save_dir,

    max_seq_len,

    batch_size,

    tokenizer,

    train_ratio=0.8,

    test_ratio=0.1,

    ):

    self.data_path = data_path

    self.save_dir = save_dir

    self.max_seq_len = max_seq_len

    self.batch_size = batch_size

    self.tokenizer = tokenizer

    self.train_ratio = train_ratio

    self.test_ratio = test_ratio

    def process(self):

    """处理数据集并保存"""

    dataset = self._make_dataset()

    dataset = self._split_dataset(dataset)

    for type in ["train", "valid", "test"]:

    self._save_dataset(dataset[type], type)

    def get_dataloader(self, type, max_examples=None):

    """获取保存的数据集，并加载 Dataloader"""

    if not os.path.exists(self.save_dir / type):

    self.process()

    dataset = self._load_dataset(type)

    if max_examples:

    indices = list(range(len(dataset)))

    random.shuffle(indices)

    max_examples = min(max_examples, len(dataset))

    indices = indices[:max_examples]

    dataset = Subset(dataset, indices)

    return DataLoader(dataset, self.batch_size, shuffle=(type == "train"))

    def _make_dataset(self):

    """处理数据集"""

    raise NotImplementedError

    def _split_dataset(self, dataset: Dataset):

    """划分数据集"""

    train_size = int(dataset.num_rows * self.train_ratio)

    dataset = dataset.train_test_split(test_size=self.test_ratio)

    dataset["train"], dataset["valid"] = (

    dataset["train"].train_test_split(train_size=train_size).values()

    )

    return dataset

    def _save_dataset(self, dataset: Dataset, type):

    """保存数据集"""

    dataset.save_to_disk(self.save_dir / type)

    def _load_dataset(self, type):

    """加载数据集"""

    dataset = load_from_disk(self.save_dir / type)

    return dataset

    class SpellCheckProcessor(Processor):

    """拼写检查任务数据预处理"""

    def _make_dataset(self):

    """处理数据集"""

    dataset = Dataset.from_generator(self._generate_examples)

    dataset = dataset.map(

    self._map_fn, batched=True, remove_columns=["text", "label"]

    )

    dataset.set_format(

    type="torch", columns=["input_ids", "attention_mask", "labels"]

    )

    return dataset

    def _generate_examples(self):

    with open(self.data_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:

    for line in f:

    if line:

    pair = line.split()

    if len(pair) == 2:

    yield {"text": pair[0], "label": pair[1]}

    def _map_fn(self, examples):

    inputs = self.tokenizer(

    examples["text"],

    max_length=self.max_seq_len,

    truncation=True,

    padding="max_length",

    return_tensors="pt",

    )

    input_ids = inputs["input_ids"]

    attention_mask = inputs["attention_mask"]

    labels = self.tokenizer(

    examples["label"],

    max_length=self.max_seq_len,

    truncation=True,

    padding="max_length",

    return_tensors="pt",

    )["input_ids"]

    labels[labels == self.tokenizer.pad_token_id] = -100

    return {

    "input_ids": input_ids,

    "attention_mask": attention_mask,

    "labels": labels,

    }

    模型训练

    src\runner\trainer.py：

    import tqdm

    import torch

    import torch.optim as optim

    from sklearn.metrics import precision_recall_fscore_support

    class Trainer:

    """训练验证与测试"""

    def __init__(self, model, device, epochs, learning_rate, checkpoint_steps=None):

    """

    参数:

  • model: 模型
  • device: 设备
  • epochs: 训练轮数
  • learning_rate: 学习率
  • checkpoint_steps: 多少步之后保存检查点

    • """

    self.model = model

    self.device = device

    self.epochs = epochs

    self.learning_rate = learning_rate

    self.checkpoint_steps = checkpoint_steps

    self.optimizer = optim.AdamW(self.model.parameters(), lr=self.learning_rate)

    def __call__(

    self,

    dataloader: dict,

    model_params_path=None,

    writer=None,

    is_test=False,

    ):

    """

    参数:

  • dataloader: 数据加载器
  • model_params_path: 模型参数保存路径
  • writer: 记录器
  • is_test: 是否执行测试

    • """

    self.dataloader = dataloader

    self.model_params_path = model_params_path

    self.writer = writer

    self.is_test = is_test

    self.model.to(self.device)

    self.global_step = 0

    测试

    if is_test:

    for k, v in self.run_epoch("test").items():

    print(f"Test {k}:", v)

    return

    训练并验证

    assert self.model_params_path is not None, "缺少模型参数保存路径"

    best_valid_loss = float("inf")

    for epoch in range(self.epochs):

    print(f"Epoch: {epoch}")

    train_metrics = self.run_epoch("train", epoch)

    for k, v in train_metrics.items():

    print(f"Train {k}:", v)

    valid_metrics = self.run_epoch("valid", epoch)

    for k, v in valid_metrics.items():

    print(f"Valid {k}:", v)

    保存最佳模型

    if valid_metrics["loss"] <= best_valid_loss:

    best_valid_loss = valid_metrics["loss"]

    torch.save(self.model.state_dict(), self.model_params_path)

    def run_epoch(self, phase, epoch=0):

    self.model.train() if phase == "train" else self.model.eval()

    初始化总损失值和总样本数

    total_loss = 0.0

    total_examples = 0

    初始化记录

    records = {}

    with torch.set_grad_enabled(phase == "train"):

    for inputs in tqdm.tqdm(self.dataloader[phase], desc=phase):

    数据转移到设备

    inputs = {k: v.to(self.device) for k, v in inputs.items()}

    前向传播

    outputs, loss = self.forward(inputs)

    反向传播和优化（仅训练阶段）

    if phase == "train":

    self.optimizer.zero_grad()

    loss.backward()

    self.optimizer.step()

    向 TensorBoard 写入损失

    if self.writer:

    self.writer.add_scalar(

    f"Loss/{phase}", loss.item(), self.global_step

    )

    self.global_step += 1

    保存模型参数

    if (

    self.checkpoint_steps

    and self.global_step % self.checkpoint_steps == 0

    ):

    checkpoint_path = str(self.model_params_path) + ".checkpoint"

    torch.save(self.model.state_dict(), checkpoint_path)

    记录损失

    current_batch_size = inputs["input_ids"].size(0)

    total_loss += loss.item() * current_batch_size

    total_examples += current_batch_size

    更新记录，用于评估

    if phase != "train":

    self.update_records(inputs, outputs, records)

    计算平均损失

    avg_loss = total_loss / total_examples

    metrics = {"loss": avg_loss}

    计算评估指标

    if phase != "train":

    self.compute_metrics(metrics, records)

    if self.writer:

    for metric_name, value in metrics.items():

    self.writer.add_scalar(f"{phase}/{metric_name}", value, epoch)

    return metrics

    def forward(self, inputs):

    """前向传播"""

    raise NotImplementedError

    def update_records(self, inputs, outputs, records):

    """更新记录"""

    raise NotImplementedError

    def compute_metrics(self, metrics, records):

    """计算评估指标"""

    raise NotImplementedError

    class SpellCheckTrainer(Trainer):

    """拼写检查任务训练器"""

    def forward(self, inputs):

    """前向传播"""

    outputs = self.model(

    input_ids=inputs["input_ids"],

    attention_mask=inputs["attention_mask"],

    labels=inputs["labels"],

    )

    return outputs, outputs["loss"]

    def update_records(self, inputs, outputs, records):

    """更新记录"""

    preds = outputs["logits"].argmax(dim=-1)

    preds[inputs["attention_mask"] != 1] = 0

    preds = preds.view(-1).detach().cpu()

    labels = inputs["labels"]

    labels[labels == -100] = 0

    labels = labels.view(-1).detach().cpu()

    input_ids = inputs["input_ids"].view(-1).detach().cpu()

    选出需要评估的部分

    mask = (input_ids != labels) | (preds != input_ids)

    preds = preds[mask]

    labels = labels[mask]

    records.setdefault("preds", []).append(preds)

    records.setdefault("labels", []).append(labels)

    def compute_metrics(self, metrics, records):

    """计算评估指标"""

    all_preds = torch.cat(records["preds"])

    all_labels = torch.cat(records["labels"])

    precision, recall, f1, _ = precision_recall_fscore_support(

    all_labels, all_preds, average="macro", zero_division=0

    )

    metrics.update({"precision": precision, "recall": recall, "f1": f1})

    工具函数

    src\models_def\utils.py：

    import torch

    def compute_parameters(model):

    """统计模型参数量"""

    print(

    f"{model.__class__.__name__}参数量:{sum(p.numel() for p in model.parameters() if p.requires_grad):,}"

    )

    def load_params(model, model_params_path):

    """加载模型参数"""

    try:

    model.load_state_dict(torch.load(model_params_path))

    except FileNotFoundError:

    print("模型参数文件不存在，使用默认参数")

    except RuntimeError:

    model.load_state_dict(torch.load(model_params_path, map_location="cpu"))

    训练流程

    src\main.py：

    import config

    from datetime import datetime

    from torch.utils.tensorboard.writer import SummaryWriter

    from runner import SpellCheckTrainer

    from preprocess import SpellCheckProcessor

    from models_def import SpellCheckModel, load_params

    learning_rate = 1e-5

    device = config.DEVICE

    bert = config.PRE_TRAINED_DIR / "bert-base-chinese"

    def model_go(task, train=None, test=None, inference=None, model_params_path=None):

    match task:

    case "spell_check":

    model = SpellCheckModel(bert)

    processor = SpellCheckProcessor(

    data_path=config.SPELL_CHECK_RAW_DATA_DIR / "data.txt",

    save_dir=config.SPELL_CHECK_PROCESSED_DATA_DIR,

    max_seq_len=256,

    batch_size=16,

    tokenizer=model.tokenizer,

    )

    trainer = SpellCheckTrainer(model, device, 1, learning_rate, 200)

    case _:

    raise ValueError("任务须为 spell_check 或 intent_classify")

    writer = None

    this_id = datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d%H%M%S")

    load_params(model, model_params_path)

    if train:

    writer = SummaryWriter(config.LOGS_DIR / f"{task}-{this_id}")

    dataloader = {

    "train": processor.get_dataloader("train"),

    "valid": processor.get_dataloader("valid"),

    }

    model_params_path = config.MODELS_DIR / f"{task}-{this_id}.pt"

    trainer(dataloader, model_params_path, writer)

    if test:

    test_dataloader = processor.get_dataloader("test")

    trainer({"test": test_dataloader}, writer=writer, is_test=True)

    if writer:

    writer.close()

    if inference:

    print(model.predict(text, device))

    text = [

    "看完那段文张，我是反对的！",

    "类似华为畅享50 Pro一洋的鸿蒙OS的千元机",

    ]

    model_go("spell_check", 1, 1, 1, config.MODELS_DIR / "spell_check.pt")

    实体抽取模型

    UIE

    UIE介绍

    UIE(Universal Information Extraction)：Yaojie Lu等人在 ACL-2022 中提出了通用信息抽取统一框架 UIE。该框架实现了实体抽取、关系抽取、事件抽取、情感分析等任务的统一建模，并使得不同任务间具备良好的迁移和泛化能力。

    UIE的核心思想是将所有信息抽取任务统一建模为文本到结构的生成过程。它巧妙地利用了预训练语言模型的强大能力，通过精心设计的“提示”（Prompt）来引导模型根据不同的需求（即“模式”或Schema）从非结构化文本中抽取出结构化的信息。

    为了方便大家使用 UIE 的强大能力，PaddleNLP 借鉴该论文的方法，基于 ERNIE 3.0 知识增强预训练模型，训练并开源了首个中文通用信息抽取模型 UIE。该模型可以支持不限定行业领域和抽取目标的关键信息抽取，实现零样本快速冷启动，并具备优秀的小样本微调能力，快速适配特定的抽取目标。

    UIE使用

    基于 uie_pytorch 项目微调并使用 UIE。

    将 uie_pytorch 拷贝到项目根目录下。在 uie_pytorch 目录下创建 test_uie.py 以演示 UIE 模型的使用。

    实体抽取

    命名实体识别（Named Entity Recognition，简称NER），是指识别文本中具有特定意义的实体。在开放域信息抽取中，抽取的类别没有限制，用户可以自己定义。

    例如抽取的目标实体类型是"时间"、"选手"和"赛事名称", 调用示例如下：

    from pprint import pprint

    from uie_predictor import UIEPredictor

    schema = ["时间", "选手", "赛事名称"]

    ie = UIEPredictor(

    model="uie-base", schema=schema, task_path="uie_pytorch/uie_base_pytorch"

    )

    pprint(

    ie(

    "2月8日上午北京冬奥会自由式滑雪女子大跳台决赛中中国选手谷爱凌以188.25分获得金牌！"

    )

    )

    [

    {

    "时间": [

    {"end": 6, "probability": 0.98573786, "start": 0, "text": "2月8日上午"}

    ],

    "赛事名称": [

    {

    "end": 23,

    "probability": 0.85030824,

    "start": 6,

    "text": "北京冬奥会自由式滑雪女子大跳台决赛",

    }

    ],

    "选手": [{"end": 31, "probability": 0.8981541, "start": 28, "text": "谷爱凌"}],

    }

    ]

    关系抽取

    关系抽取（Relation Extraction，简称RE），是指从文本中识别实体并抽取实体之间的语义关系，进而获取三元组信息，即<主体，谓语，客体>。

    例如以"竞赛名称"作为抽取主体，抽取关系类型为"主办方"、"承办方"和"已举办次数", 调用示例如下：

    from pprint import pprint

    from uie_predictor import UIEPredictor

    schema = {"竞赛名称": ["主办方", "承办方", "已举办次数"]}

    ie = UIEPredictor(

    model="uie-base", schema=schema, task_path="uie_pytorch/uie_base_pytorch"

    )

    pprint(

    ie(

    "2022语言与智能技术竞赛由中国中文信息学会和中国计算机学会联合主办，百度公司、中国中文信息学会评测工作委员会和中国计算机学会自然语言处理专委会承办，已连续举办4届，成为全球最热门的中文NLP赛事之一。"

    )

    )

    [

    {

    "竞赛名称": [

    {

    "end": 13,

    "probability": 0.7825393,

    "relations": {

    "主办方": [

    UIEPredictor 参数说明

    model：选择任务使用的模型，默认为uie-base，可选有uie-base, uie-medium, uie-mini, uie-micro, uie-nano和uie-medical-base, uie-base-en。

    schema：定义任务抽取目标，可参考开箱即用中不同任务的调用示例进行配置。

    schema_lang：设置schema的语言，默认为zh, 可选有zh和en。因为中英schema的构造有所不同，因此需要指定schema的语言。该参数只对uie-m-base和uie-m-large模型有效。

    batch_size：批处理大小，请结合机器情况进行调整，默认为1。

    task_path：设定自定义的模型。

    position_prob：模型对于span的起始位置/终止位置的结果概率在0~1之间，返回结果去掉小于这个阈值的结果，默认为0.5，span的最终概率输出为起始位置概率和终止位置概率的乘积。

    use_fp16：是否使用fp16进行加速，默认关闭。fp16推理速度更快。如果选择fp16，请先确保机器正确安装NVIDIA相关驱动和基础软件，确保CUDA>=11.2，cuDNN>=8.1.1，初次使用需按照提示安装相关依赖。其次，需要确保GPU设备的CUDA计算能力（CUDA Compute Capability）大于7.0。

    模型架构说明

    模型推理过程

    如果预测目标是实体，UIE 会直接返回预测的实体结果。

    如果预测目标是关系，UIE会先预测关系的主体，并将关系主体的预测结果和关系名称拼接，作为一个新的 prompt 输入模型来预测关系的客体。

    数据标注

    doccano安装与启动

    doccano 是 documment anotation 的缩写，是一个开源的文本标注工具，我们可以用它为NLP任务的语料库进行标注，支持情感分析、命名实体识别、文本摘要等任务。

    使用 doccano 进行数据标注，为了安装 doccano，创建 Python3.8 的虚拟环境：

    conda create -n doccano python=3.8

    conda activate doccano

    pip install doccano

    初始化数据库：

    doccano init

    创建一个超级用户：

    doccano createuser --username doccano --password doccano

    启动 Web 服务：

    doccano webserver --port 8000

    在另一个终端窗口中启动任务队列以处理文件的上传与下载：

    doccano task

    进入 http://127.0.0.1:8000/zh 页面。

    点击快速开始，输入账号密码登录。

    创建任务

    点击左上角的创建，创建一个序列标注任务。

    填写项目信息。

    勾选选项，点击创建。

    导入数据集

    数据集—>操作—>导入数据集。

    文件格式选择 TextLine 格式，点击下方灰色区域选择并上传文件，之后点击导入。

    定义标签

    标签—>操作—>创建标签，即可逐个添加标签。

    也可以通过 json 文件的方式导入文件中定义好的标签。

    选择导入标签，选择 lable_config.json 文件，点击导入。

    点击 Relation 可逐个添加关系，也可以通过文件的方式导入关系。

    标注

    点击开始标注，按住鼠标左键选中文本后松开左键，弹出标签选项，选择标签进行标注。

    一条样本标注完成后点击左上角 × 将其改为 ✓ 将样本标记为完成状态。

    点击右上角 > 标注下一条样本。

    如果要标注关系，需要点击右下角 Label Types 下的选项将其切换为 Relation。勾选要标注的关系，依次点击关系主体标签和关系客体标签来标注一个关系。

    导出数据集

    全部样本标注完成后，点击数据集—>操作—>导出数据集。

    选择导出格式为 JSONL，点击导出。

    解压导出文件，其中的 .jsonl 文件就是标注后的数据文件。

    数据格式转换

    doccano 标注后的数据无法直接作为模型微调的原始数据，需要对数据的格式进行转换。并适当添加负例。

    通过向原始数据中添加负例，能够让模型学习到 prompt 在文本里找不到结果的情形。模型如果从未见过一个 prompt 下没有结果的情况，容易在推理时产生误预测。加入负例让模型学会预测空集合的合法性，从而更稳健地进行抽取。在标注数据不足或类别不均衡的情况下，加入负例也可以增加训练数据多样性，防止模型只记住正例的模式。

    doccano 标注后的数据格式

    doccano 标注后的数据格式如下：

    text 为一条文本。

    entities 中包含该条文本所有的实体：

    id 表示该实体对应的序号

    label 表示实体名称

    start_offset 表示该实体的起始位置

    end_offset 表示该实体的结束位置

    注意这里的 start_offset 和 end_offset 构成的是一个左闭右开区间。

    relations 中包含该条文本所有的关系：

    from_id 代表关系的主体所对应的实体 id

    to_id 代表关系的客体所对应的实体 id

    type 表示关系名称

    为什么需要转换数据格式

    模型微调时的输入需要是一段 prompt 和一段 content，以及一个 result_list 结果列表：

    prompt 是一个实体名称或者是“主体+关系名称”的格式

    content 是一条文本

    result_list 中应包含文本中一类实体或关系 prompt 对应的结果的开始和结束位置

    模型微调需要的原始数据格式如下：

    因此 doccano 标注后的数据无法直接作为模型微调的原始数据。

    对于实体 prompt，我们需要将原本一条数据中一个文本对应的多个实体；拆分为多条数据，每条数据中一个文本对应一个实体。

    对于关系 prompt，我们同样需要将其从一对多拆分为一对一，并将每个关系拼接成“关系主体 + 的 + 关系名称”的形式。

    因此需要对数据的格式进行转换，在转换的同时添加负例。

    执行数据格式转换

    doccano.py 数据转换脚本参数说明：

    doccano_file: 从doccano导出的数据标注文件。

    save_dir: 训练数据的保存目录，默认存储在data目录下。

    negative_ratio: 最大负例比例，该参数只对抽取类型任务有效，适当构造负例可提升模型效果。负例数量和实际的标签数量有关，最大负例数量 = negative_ratio * 正例数量。该参数只对训练集有效，默认为5。为了保证评估指标的准确性，验证集和测试集默认构造全负例。

    splits: 划分数据集时训练集、验证集所占的比例。默认为[0.8, 0.1, 0.1]表示按照8:1:1的比例将数据划分为训练集、验证集和测试集。

    task_type: 选择任务类型，可选有抽取和分类两种类型的任务。

    options: 指定分类任务的类别标签，该参数只对分类类型任务有效。默认为["正向", "负向"]。

    prompt_prefix: 声明分类任务的prompt前缀信息，该参数只对分类类型任务有效。默认为"情感倾向"。

    is_shuffle: 是否对数据集进行随机打散，默认为True。

    seed: 随机种子，默认为1000.

    separator: 实体类别/评价维度与分类标签的分隔符，该参数只对实体/评价维度级分类任务有效。默认为"##"。

    使用脚本转换 doccano 标注后的数据为模型微调的原始数据：

    python uie_pytorch/doccano.py --doccano_file ./data/uie/doccano/doccano.jsonl --save_dir ./data/uie/raw

    转换原理（了解）

    数据预处理（了解）

    模型微调

    finetune.py 模型微调脚本参数说明：

    train_path: 训练集文件路径。

    dev_path: 验证集文件路径。

    save_dir: 模型存储路径，默认为./checkpoint。

    learning_rate: 学习率，默认为1e-5。

    batch_size: 批处理大小，请结合机器情况进行调整，默认为16。

    max_seq_len: 文本最大切分长度，输入超过最大长度时会对输入文本进行自动切分，默认为512。

    num_epochs: 训练轮数，默认为100。

    model: 选择模型，程序会基于选择的模型进行模型微调，默认为uie_base_pytorch。

    seed: 随机种子，默认为1000.

    logging_steps: 日志打印的间隔steps数，默认10。

    valid_steps: evaluate的间隔steps数，默认100。

    device: 选用什么设备进行训练，可选cpu或gpu。

    max_model_num: 保存的模型的个数，不包含model_best和early_stopping保存的模型，默认为5。

    early_stopping: 是否采用早停（Early Stopping），默认不使用。

    python uie_pytorch/finetune.py --train_path "./data/uie/raw/train.txt" --dev_path "./data/uie/raw/dev.txt" --save_dir "./uie_pytorch/checkpoint" --learning_rate 1e-5 --batch_size 8 --max_seq_len 512 --num_epochs 10 --model "uie_pytorch/uie_base_pytorch" --seed 1000 --logging_steps 50 --valid_steps 200 --device "gpu"

    模型评估

    获取 span 并进行评估

    获取模型两个线性层的输出作为开始位置和结束位置的预测，并对输出进行 sigmoid 计算，获取大于阈值的元素的索引位置，组成开始位置列表和结束位置列表。

    将每个结束位置与在它之前且距离最近的开始位置相匹配，组成一个 span。

    通过双指针遍历开始位置列表和结束位置列表，获取 span 列表。

    得到预测结果组成的 span 列表，和标签组成的 span 列表。

    使用两个列表计算精确率、召回率、F1分数指标来评估模型。

    执行模型评估

    evaluate.py 模型评估脚本参数说明：

    model_path: 进行评估的模型文件夹路径，路径下需包含模型权重文件pytorch_model.bin及配置文件config.json。

    test_path: 进行评估的测试集文件。

    batch_size: 批处理大小，请结合机器情况进行调整，默认为16。

    max_seq_len: 文本最大切分长度，输入超过最大长度时会对输入文本进行自动切分，默认为512。

    device: 选用进行训练的设备，可选cpu或gpu。

    python uie_pytorch/evaluate.py --model_path ./uie_pytorch/checkpoint/model_best --test_path ./data/uie/raw/test.txt --batch_size 8 --max_seq_len 512

    "localhost",

    f"-p{config.MYSQL_CONFIG['password']}",

    ]

    try:

    创建数据库

    print(f"创建 {config.MYSQL_CONFIG['database']} 数据库")

    subprocess.run(

    mysql_cmd_prefix

    + [

    "-e",

    f"DROP DATABASE IF EXISTS {config.MYSQL_CONFIG['database']}; CREATE DATABASE {config.MYSQL_CONFIG['database']};",

    ],

    stderr=subprocess.PIPE,

    )

    stdin=sql_file,

    check=True,

    stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL,

    )

    print("数据导入成功")

    except subprocess.CalledProcessError as e:

    print(f"导入失败，错误: {e.stderr}")

    def query_data_from_mysql() -> dict[str, list[dict]]:

    """查询 MySQL 数据"""

    结果集

    with mysql_conn.cursor(pymysql.cursors.DictCursor) as cursor:

    查询 单品、商品、品牌、三级分类、二级分类、一级分类

    cursor.execute(

    "select "

    "sku_info.id as sku_id,"  # 单品ID

    "sku_info.sku_name as sku_name,"  # 单品名称

    "sku_info.sku_desc as sku_desc,"  # 单品描述

    "sku_info.price as price,"  # 单品价格

    "spu_info.id as spu_id,"  # 商品ID

    "spu_info.spu_name as spu_name,"  # 商品名称

    "base_category3.name as category3_name,"  # 三级分类

    "base_category2.name as category2_name,"  # 二级分类

    "base_category1.name as category1_name,"  # 一级分类

    "base_trademark.tm_name as trademark_name"  # 品牌名称

    """

    from sku_info

    left join spu_info on sku_info.spu_id = spu_info.id

    left join base_category3 on sku_info.category3_id = base_category3.id

    left join base_category2 on base_category3.category2_id = base_category2.id

    left join base_category1 on base_category2.category1_id = base_category1.id

    left join base_trademark on sku_info.tm_id = base_trademark.id;

    """

    )

    mysql_data["skus"] = cursor.fetchall()

    查询 sku_id、商品属性

    cursor.execute(

    "select "

    "sku_id as sku_id,"  # sku_id

    "attr_name as attr_type,"  # 属性类型

    "value_name as attr_name"  # 属性值

    " from sku_attr_value;"

    )

    mysql_data["sku_attrs"] = cursor.fetchall()

    查询 sku_id、商品销售属性

    cursor.execute(

    "select "

    "sku_id as sku_id,"  # sku_id

    "sale_attr_name as attr_type,"  # 属性类型

    "sale_attr_value_name as attr_name"  # 属性值

    " from sku_sale_attr_value;"

    )

    查询 sku_id、详情图片 URL

    cursor.execute(

    "select "

    "sku_info.id as sku_id,"  # sku_id

    "sku_image.img_url as sku_image_url"  # 详情图片 URL

    " from sku_info "

    "left join sku_image on sku_info.id = sku_image.sku_id;"

    )

    mysql_data["sku_image_urls"] = cursor.fetchall()

    查询 用户浏览日志

    cursor.execute(

    "select "

    "user_id,"  # 用户ID

    "sku_id,"  # sku_id

    "view_time"  # 浏览时间

    " from user_view_log;"

    )

    mysql_data["user_view_logs"] = cursor.fetchall()

    return mysql_data

    def get_info_from_images(mysql_data: dict):

    """获取商品详情图片信息"""

    sku_image_descs: dict[int, list[str]] = {}

    指定语言为中文，并使用 GPU 加速

    reader = easyocr.Reader(["ch_sim", "en"], gpu=True)

    for url in mysql_data.get("sku_image_urls", []):

    if url["sku_image_url"].startswith("/data"):

    print(f"processing {url['sku_image_url']}...")

    image_url = str(config.BASE_DIR / url["sku_image_url"].lstrip("/"))

    results = reader.readtext(image_url)

    text = "".join([result[1] for result in results])

    sku_image_descs.setdefault(url["sku_id"], []).append(text)

    mysql_data["sku_image_descs"] = sku_image_descs

    def spell_check(mysql_data, model_params_path):

    sku_image_desc_list, config.DEVICE

    )

    sku_image_descs[sku_id] = checked_desc_list

    print(sku_id)

    for i, j in zip(sku_image_desc_list, checked_desc_list):

    print("origin_desc:", i)

    print("checked_desc:", j)

    print()

    基于模型的实体抽取

    src\entity_extractor_model_base.py：

    import sys

    import config

    from neo4j import GraphDatabase

    from config import NEO4J_URI, NEO4J_AUTH

    将 uie_pytorch 加入路径

    sys.path.insert(0, str(config.BASE_DIR / "uie_pytorch"))

    from uie_predictor import UIEPredictor

    class EntityExtractorModelBase:

    """实体抽取-基于模型:使用 UIE 抽取实体"""

    def __init__(self, model_params_path, device="cpu"):

    初始化 UIE

    self.uie = UIEPredictor(

    model="uie-base",

    task_path=model_params_path,

    schema=[],

    engine="pytorch",

    device=device,

    )

    初始化所有属性类型

    self.attr_types = set()

    with GraphDatabase.driver(uri=NEO4J_URI, auth=NEO4J_AUTH) as driver:

    for record in driver.execute_query("MATCH (attr:Attr) RETURN attr.type")[0]:

    self.attr_types.add(record["attr.type"])

    self.attr_types = list(self.attr_types)

    self.attr_types.remove("价格")

    def __call__(self, text, schema, batch_size=8):

    include_attr = "属性" in schema

    if include_attr:

    schema.remove("属性")

    schema.extend(self.attr_types)

    self.uie.set_schema(schema)

    res: list[dict] = []

    统一转换为列表

    input_texts = text if isinstance(text, list) else [text]

    逐批次处理

    for i in range(0, len(input_texts), batch_size):

    batch_texts = input_texts[i : i + batch_size]

    batch_res = self.uie(batch_texts)

    for one_res in batch_res:

    for k, values in one_res.items():

    one_res[k] = list({v["text"] for v in values})

    res.extend(batch_res)

    将属性归集到属性字段下

    if include_attr:

    for one_res in res:

    for attr_type in self.attr_types:

    if attr_type in one_res:

    one_res.setdefault("属性", []).extend(one_res.pop(attr_type))

    one_res["属性"] = list(set(one_res["属性"]))

    return res if isinstance(text, list) else res[0]

    if __name__ == "__main__":

    eerb = EntityExtractorModelBase(

    config.BASE_DIR / "uie_pytorch/checkpoint/model_best", "gpu"

    )

    res = eerb(

    "100元的红色或黑色颜色的小米手机8G内存的还有货吗",

    ["品类", "品牌", "属性", "商品属性类型", "价格"],

    )

    print(res)

    对商品详情信息进行实体抽取

    def entity_extract(mysql_data, schema):

    """对商品详情信息进行实体抽取"""

    eerb = EntityExtractorModelBase(

    config.BASE_DIR / "uie_pytorch/checkpoint/model_best", "gpu"

    )

    获取每个单品详情中的所有属性值

    sku_desc_attrs: dict[int, dict[str, list]] = {}

    for skus in mysql_data.get("skus", []):

    sku_id = skus["sku_id"]

    sku_desc = skus["sku_desc"]

    desc_attrs: dict[str, list] = {}

    抽取该个单品对应的所有详情

    res_dict = eerb(sku_desc, schema)

    添加抽取结果

    for attr_type, attr_name in res_dict.items():

    desc_attrs[attr_type] = list(set(attr_name))

    sku_desc_attrs[sku_id] = desc_attrs

    mysql_data["sku_desc_attrs"] = sku_desc_attrs

    获取每个单品对应的图片详情中的所有属性值

    sku_image_desc_attrs: dict[int, dict[str, list]] = {}

    for sku_id, sku_image_desc_list in mysql_data.get("sku_image_descs", {}).items():

    image_desc_attrs: dict[str, list] = {}  # 每个单品对应的所有图片详情中的所有属性

    抽取该个单品对应的所有图片详情

    res_dict_list = eerb(sku_image_desc_list, schema)

    添加抽取结果

    for res_dict in res_dict_list:

    for attr_type, attr_name in res_dict.items():

    image_desc_attrs.setdefault(attr_type, []).extend(attr_name)

    对每个属性的属性值去重

    for attr_type in image_desc_attrs:

    image_desc_attrs[attr_type] = list(set(image_desc_attrs[attr_type]))

    sku_image_desc_attrs[sku_id] = image_desc_attrs

    mysql_data["sku_image_desc_attrs"] = sku_image_desc_attrs

    导入数据到 Neo4j

    将数据以如下结构存入 Neo4j：

    导入数据到 Neo4j

    def import_data_2_neo4j(mysql_data):

    """导入数据到 Neo4j"""

    def create_sku_spu_cat3_cat2_cat1(driver: Driver, sku: dict):

    """

    创建

    (单品)-[:BELONG]->(商品)-[:BELONG]->(三级分类)-[:BELONG]->(二级分类)-[:BELONG]->(一级分类)

    (商品)-[:BELONG]->(品牌)

    """

    driver.execute_query(

    """

    MERGE (sku:SKU {sku_id: $sku_id, name: $sku_name})

    MERGE (spu:SPU {spu_id: $spu_id, name: $spu_name})

    MERGE (trademark:Trademark {name: $trademark_name})

    MERGE (cat3:Category3 {name: $cat3_name})

    MERGE (cat2:Category2 {name: $cat2_name})

    MERGE (cat1:Category1 {name: $cat1_name})

    MERGE (price:Attr {type: "价格", name: $price})

    MERGE (sku)-[:BELONG]->(spu)

    MERGE (spu)-[:BELONG]->(trademark)

    MERGE (spu)-[:BELONG]->(cat3)

    MERGE (cat3)-[:BELONG]->(cat2)

    MERGE (cat2)-[:BELONG]->(cat1)

    MERGE (sku)-[:HAVE]->(price)

    """,

    parameters_={

    "sku_id": sku["sku_id"],

    "sku_name": sku["sku_name"],

    "spu_id": sku["spu_id"],

    "spu_name": sku["spu_name"],

    "trademark_name": sku["trademark_name"],

    "cat3_name": sku["category3_name"],

    "cat2_name": sku["category2_name"],

    "cat1_name": sku["category1_name"],

    "price": float(sku["price"]),

    },

    )

    def create_sku_attr(driver: Driver, sku_attr: dict):

    """创建 (单品)-[:HAVE]->(单品属性)"""

    driver.execute_query(

    """

    OPTIONAL MATCH (sku:SKU {sku_id: $sku_id})

    WITH sku

    WHERE sku IS NOT NULL

    MERGE (attr:Attr {name: $attr_name, type: $attr_type})

    MERGE (sku)-[:HAVE]->(attr)

    """,

    parameters_={

    "sku_id": sku_attr["sku_id"],

    "attr_name": sku_attr["attr_name"],

    "attr_type": sku_attr["attr_type"],

    },

    )

    def create_user_view_sku(driver: Driver, user_view_log: dict):

    """创建 (用户)-[:VIEW]->(单品)"""

    driver.execute_query(

    """

    MERGE (user:User {user_id: $user_id})

    WITH user

    OPTIONAL MATCH (sku:SKU {sku_id: $sku_id})

    WITH user, sku

    WHERE sku IS NOT NULL

    CREATE (user)-[:VIEW {view_time: $view_time}]->(sku)

    """,

    parameters_={

    "user_id": user_view_log["user_id"],

    "sku_id": user_view_log["sku_id"],

    "view_time": user_view_log["view_time"],

    },

    )

    def create_sku_desc_attr(

    driver: Driver, sku_desc_attrs: dict[int, dict[str, list]]

    ):

    """创建 (单品)-[:HAVE]->(单品属性)"""

    for sku_id, attrs in sku_desc_attrs.items():

    for attr_type, attr_names in attrs.items():

    driver.execute_query(

    """

    OPTIONAL MATCH (sku:SKU {sku_id: $sku_id})

    WITH sku

    WHERE sku IS NOT NULL

    AND NOT (sku)-[:HAVE]->(:Attr {type: $attr_type})

    MERGE (attr:Attr {name: $attr_name, type: $attr_type})

    MERGE (sku)-[:HAVE]->(attr)

    """,

    parameters_={

    "sku_id": sku_id,

    "attr_name": attr_names[-1],

    "attr_type": attr_type,

    },

    )

    print(f"✅ ({sku_id})-[:HAVE]->({attr_type}:{attr_names[-1]})")

    连接 Neo4j

    with GraphDatabase.driver(config.NEO4J_URI, auth=config.NEO4J_AUTH) as driver:

    删除所有约束

    records, _, _ = driver.execute_query("SHOW CONSTRAINTS")

    constraints = [record["name"] for record in records]

    for constraint in constraints:

    driver.execute_query(f"DROP CONSTRAINT {constraint} IF EXISTS")

    清空图表

    driver.execute_query("MATCH (n) DETACH DELETE n")

    创建属性唯一性约束

    for constraint in [

    "CREATE CONSTRAINT sku_id IF NOT EXISTS FOR (sku:SKU) REQUIRE sku.sku_id IS UNIQUE",  # SKU.sku_id 属性唯一性约束

    "CREATE CONSTRAINT spu_id IF NOT EXISTS FOR (spu:SPU) REQUIRE spu.spu_id IS UNIQUE",  # SPU.spu_id 属性唯一性约束

    "CREATE CONSTRAINT cat3_name IF NOT EXISTS FOR (cat3:Category3) REQUIRE cat3.name IS UNIQUE",  # Category3.name 属性唯一性约束

    "CREATE CONSTRAINT cat2_name IF NOT EXISTS FOR (cat2:Category2) REQUIRE cat2.name IS UNIQUE",  # Category2.name 属性唯一性约束

    "CREATE CONSTRAINT cat1_name IF NOT EXISTS FOR (cat1:Category1) REQUIRE cat1.name IS UNIQUE",  # Category1.name 属性唯一性约束

    "CREATE CONSTRAINT trademark_name IF NOT EXISTS FOR (trademark:Trademark) REQUIRE trademark.name IS UNIQUE",  # Trademark.name 属性唯一性约束

    "CREATE CONSTRAINT attr IF NOT EXISTS FOR (attr:Attr) REQUIRE (attr.name, attr.type) IS UNIQUE",  # (Attr.name, Attr.type) 属性唯一性约束

    "CREATE CONSTRAINT user_id IF NOT EXISTS FOR (user:User) REQUIRE user.user_id IS UNIQUE",  # User.user_id 属性唯一性约束

    ]:

    driver.execute_query(constraint)

    print("✅ 属性唯一性约束创建成功")

    添加节点和关系

    for sku in mysql_data.get("skus", []):

    create_sku_spu_cat3_cat2_cat1(driver, sku)

    print(

    "✅ "

    f"({sku['sku_name']})-[:BELONG]->"

    f"({sku['spu_name']})-[:BELONG]->"

    f"({sku['category3_name']})-[:BELONG]->"

    f"({sku['category2_name']})-[:BELONG]->"

    f"({sku['category1_name']})"

    )

    print(f"✅ ({sku['spu_name']})-[:BELONG]->({sku['trademark_name']})")

    for sku_attr in mysql_data.get("sku_attrs", []):

    create_sku_attr(driver, sku_attr)

    print(

    "✅ "

    f"({sku_attr['sku_id']})-[:HAVE]->"

    f"({{{sku_attr['attr_type']}:{sku_attr['attr_name']}}})"

    )

    for sku_sale_attr in mysql_data.get("sku_sale_attrs", []):

    create_sku_attr(driver, sku_sale_attr)

    print(

    "✅ "

    f"({sku_sale_attr['sku_id']})-[:HAVE]->"

    f"({{{sku_sale_attr['attr_type']}:{sku_sale_attr['attr_name']}}})"

    )

    for user_view_log in mysql_data.get("user_view_logs", []):

    create_user_view_sku(driver, user_view_log)

    print(

    "✅ "

    f"({user_view_log['user_id']})-"

    f"[:VIEW ({user_view_log['view_time']})]->"

    f"({user_view_log['sku_id']})"

    )

    create_sku_desc_attr(driver, mysql_data.get("sku_desc_attrs", {}))

    create_sku_desc_attr(driver, mysql_data.get("sku_image_desc_attrs", {}))

    意图识别模型

    该项目中意图识别模型是一个单标签多分类模型，用于对用户输入进行意图分类，并在对话过程中根据意图确定抽取那些实体、查询哪些数据、以及如何回复用户。

    模型定义

    src\models_def\intent_classify.py：

    import torch

    import torch.nn as nn

    from transformers import AutoTokenizer, BertModel

    class IntentClassifyModel(nn.Module):

    """意图分类模型"""

    def __init__(self, model_name: str, intent_list: list):

    super().__init__()

    self.intent_list = intent_list

    self.tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(model_name)

    self.model = BertModel.from_pretrained(model_name)

    hidden_size = self.model.config.hidden_size

    self.classifier = nn.Linear(hidden_size, len(self.intent_list))

    self.loss_fn = nn.CrossEntropyLoss()

    def forward(self, input_ids, attention_mask=None, labels=None):

    outputs = self.model(input_ids, attention_mask)

    logits = self.classifier(outputs.last_hidden_state[:, 0, :])

    loss = 0.0

    if labels is not None:

    loss += self.loss_fn(logits, labels)

    return {"logits": logits, "loss": loss}

    @torch.inference_mode()

    def predict(self, text: str | list[str], device=torch.device("cpu"), batch_size=8):

    self.eval()

    self.to(device)

    res: list[str] = []

    统一转换为列表

    input_texts = text if isinstance(text, list) else [text]

    逐批次处理

    for i in range(0, len(input_texts), batch_size):

    batch_texts = input_texts[i : i + batch_size]

    inputs = self.tokenizer(

    batch_texts,

    max_length=256,

    truncation=True,

    padding=True,

    return_tensors="pt",

    ).to(device)

    outputs = self(inputs["input_ids"], inputs["attention_mask"])

    preds = torch.argmax(outputs["logits"], dim=1)

    batch_res = [self.intent_list[int(pred.item())] for pred in preds]

    res.extend(batch_res)

    return res if isinstance(text, list) else res[0]

    数据预处理

    src\preprocess\process.py：

    class IntentClassifyProcessor(Processor):

    """意图分类任务数据预处理"""

    def __init__(

    self,

    data_path,

    save_dir,

    max_seq_len,

    batch_size,

    tokenizer,

    intent_list,

    train_ratio=0.8,

    test_ratio=0.1,

    ):

    super().__init__(

    data_path,

    save_dir,

    max_seq_len,

    batch_size,

    tokenizer,

    train_ratio,

    test_ratio,

    )

    self.intent_list = intent_list

    self.intent_2_idx = {intent: idx for idx, intent in enumerate(self.intent_list)}

    def _make_dataset(self):

    """处理数据集"""

    dataset = Dataset.from_csv(str(self.data_path))

    dataset = dataset.map(

    self._map_fn, batched=True, remove_columns=["text", "intent"]

    )

    dataset.set_format(

    type="torch", columns=["input_ids", "attention_mask", "labels"]

    )

    return dataset

    def _map_fn(self, examples):

    inputs = self.tokenizer(

    examples["text"],

    max_length=self.max_seq_len,

    truncation=True,

    padding="max_length",

    return_tensors="pt",

    )

    input_ids = inputs["input_ids"]

    attention_mask = inputs["attention_mask"]

    intents = examples["intent"]

    labels = [self.intent_2_idx[i] for i in intents]

    return {

    "input_ids": input_ids,

    "attention_mask": attention_mask,

    "labels": labels,

    }

    模型训练

    src\runner\trainer.py：

    class IntentClassifyTrainer(Trainer):

    """意图分类任务训练器"""

    def forward(self, inputs):

    """前向传播"""

    outputs = self.model(

    input_ids=inputs["input_ids"],

    attention_mask=inputs["attention_mask"],

    labels=inputs["labels"],

    )

    return outputs, outputs["loss"]

    def update_records(self, inputs, outputs, records):

    """更新记录"""

    preds = outputs["logits"].argmax(dim=-1).detach().cpu()

    labels = inputs["labels"].detach().cpu()

    records.setdefault("preds", []).append(preds)

    records.setdefault("labels", []).append(labels)

    def compute_metrics(self, metrics, records):

    """计算评估指标"""

    all_preds = torch.cat(records["preds"])

    all_labels = torch.cat(records["labels"])

    precision, recall, f1, _ = precision_recall_fscore_support(

    all_labels, all_preds, average="macro", zero_division=0

    )

    metrics.update({"precision": precision, "recall": recall, "f1": f1})

    训练流程

    补充src\main.py：

    import config

    from datetime import datetime

    from torch.utils.tensorboard.writer import SummaryWriter

    from runner import SpellCheckTrainer, IntentClassifyTrainer

    from preprocess import SpellCheckProcessor, IntentClassifyProcessor

    from models_def import SpellCheckModel, IntentClassifyModel, load_params

    learning_rate = 1e-5

    device = config.DEVICE

    bert = config.PRE_TRAINED_DIR / "bert-base-chinese"

    def model_go(

    task,

    train=None,

    test=None,

    write=None,

    inference=None,

    model_params_path=None,

    ):

    match task:

    case "spell_check":

    model = SpellCheckModel(bert)

    processor = SpellCheckProcessor(

    data_path=config.SPELL_CHECK_RAW_DATA_DIR / "data.txt",

    save_dir=config.SPELL_CHECK_PROCESSED_DATA_DIR,

    max_seq_len=256,

    batch_size=16,

    tokenizer=model.tokenizer,

    )

    trainer = SpellCheckTrainer(model, device, 1, learning_rate, 200)

    case "intent_classify":

    model = IntentClassifyModel(bert, config.INTENT)

    processor = IntentClassifyProcessor(

    data_path=config.INTENT_CLASSIFY_RAW_DATA_DIR / "data.csv",

    save_dir=config.INTENT_CLASSIFY_PROCESSED_DATA_DIR,

    max_seq_len=256,

    batch_size=16,

    tokenizer=model.tokenizer,

    intent_list=config.INTENT,

    )

    trainer = IntentClassifyTrainer(model, device, 10, learning_rate)

    case _:

    raise ValueError("任务须为 spell_check 或 intent_classify")

    writer = None

    this_id = datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d%H%M%S")

    load_params(model, model_params_path)

    if train:

    if write and not writer:

    writer = SummaryWriter(config.LOGS_DIR / f"{task}-{this_id}")

    dataloader = {

    "train": processor.get_dataloader("train"),

    "valid": processor.get_dataloader("valid"),

    }

    model_params_path = config.MODELS_DIR / f"{task}-{this_id}.pt"

    trainer(dataloader, model_params_path, writer)

    if test:

    if write and not writer:

    writer = SummaryWriter(config.LOGS_DIR / f"{task}-{this_id}")

    test_dataloader = processor.get_dataloader("test", 1000)

    trainer({"test": test_dataloader}, writer=writer, is_test=True)

    if writer:

    writer.close()

    if inference:

    print(model.predict(text, device))

    text = [

    "看完那段文张，我是反对的！",

    "类似华为畅享50 Pro一洋的鸿蒙OS的千元机",

    ]

    model_go(

    "spell_check",

    1,

    1,

    1,

    1,

    config.MODELS_DIR / "spell_check.pt",

    )

    model_go(

    "intent_classify",

    1,

    1,

    1,

    1,

    config.MODELS_DIR / "intent_classify.pt",

    )

    对话处理流程

    基于规则的意图识别

    src\intent_recognize_rule_base.py：

    import re

    class IntentRecognizeRuleBase:

    """意图识别-规则"""

    def __init__(self):

    同义词词典

    self.synonyms = {

    "商品": [

    "商品",

    "产品",

    "型号",

    "款式",

    "机型",

    "版本",

    "单品",

    "手机",

    "电脑",

    "手表",

    "平板",

    "鞋子",

    "服装",

    ],

    "属性": [

    "属性",

    "参数",

    "规格",

    "配置",

    "功能",

    "特点",

    "内存",

    "屏幕",

    "颜色",

    "尺寸",

    "重量",

    "防水",

    "5g",

    "容量",

    ],

    "品类": [

    "品类",

    "类型",

    "种类",

    "类别",

    "产品线",

    "电脑",

    "手机",

    "手表",

    "平板",

    "运动鞋",

    "服装",

    ],

    "品牌": [

    "品牌",

    "牌子",

    "厂商",

    "公司",

    "厂家",

    "华为",

    "苹果",

    "小米",

    "三星",

    "耐克",

    ],

    "所有": [

    "所有",

    "全部",

    "哪些",

    "有什么",

    "有哪",

    "有哪些",

    "什么选择",

    "什么型号",

    ],

    "价格": ["价格", "价位", "多少钱", "报价", "售价", "元", "块", "价位段"],

    }

    def __call__(self, text):

    保留中文和数字，统一小写

    text = re.sub(r"[^\u4e00-\u9fa5a-zA-Z0-9\s]", "", text).lower()

    规则1：查询和某商品某个属性具有相同属性值的其他商品

    if re.search(r"(和|与|同).+(相同|一样|同款).+(商品|手机|电脑|手表)", text):

    return "查询和某商品某个属性具有相同属性值的其他商品"

    规则2：查询某品类某品牌具有某些属性的单品

    if (

    (self.has_category_word(text) or self.has_word("品类", text))

    and self.has_brand_word(text)

    and self.has_word("属性", text)

    ):

    return "查询某品类某品牌具有某些属性的单品"

    规则3：查询某品类某品牌某价格区间的单品

    if (

    (self.has_category_word(text) or self.has_word("品类", text))

    and self.has_brand_word(text)

    and self.has_price_range(text)

    ):

    return "查询某品类某品牌某价格区间的单品"

    规则4：查询某商品具有某些属性值的单品的价格

    if (

    self.has_word("属性", text) or self.has_attribute_value(text)

    ) and self.has_word("价格", text):

    return "查询某商品具有某些属性值的单品的价格"

    规则5：查询某品类某价格区间的单品

    if (

    (self.has_category_word(text) or self.has_word("品类", text))

    and self.has_price_range(text)

    and not self.has_brand_word(text)

    ):

    return "查询某品类某价格区间的单品"

    规则6：查询某商品具有某些属性值的单品

    if (

    self.has_word("属性", text) or self.has_attribute_value(text)

    ) and self.has_word("所有", text):

    return "查询某商品具有某些属性值的单品"

    规则7：查询某品类具有某些属性的单品

    if (

    (self.has_category_word(text) or self.has_word("品类", text))

    and self.has_word("属性", text)

    and not self.has_brand_word(text)

    ):

    return "查询某品类具有某些属性的单品"

    规则8：查询某品类某品牌的所有商品

    if (

    (self.has_category_word(text) or self.has_word("品类", text))

    and self.has_brand_word(text)

    and self.has_word("所有", text)

    ):

    return "查询某品类某品牌的所有商品"

    规则9：查询某商品的某个属性的属性值

    if re.search(

    r".+的.+(是多少|是多大|是什么|什么尺寸|什么颜色|多大内存|多大屏幕)",

    text,

    ):

    return "查询某商品的某个属性的属性值"

    规则10：查询某品类某个属性的所有属性值

    if (

    (self.has_category_word(text) or self.has_word("品类", text))

    and self.has_word("属性", text)

    and self.has_word("所有", text)

    ):

    return "查询某品类某个属性的所有属性值"

    规则11：查询某品牌所有品类

    if self.has_brand_word(text) and re.search(r"有哪些类型|什么品类", text):

    return "查询某品牌所有品类"

    规则12：查询某品类所有品牌

    if self.has_category_word(text) and re.search(r"有哪些品牌|什么牌子", text):

    return "查询某品类所有品牌"

    规则13：查询某品类所有商品

    if self.has_category_word(text) and re.search(

    r"所有商品|全部产品|有哪些型号", text

    ):

    return "查询某品类所有商品"

    规则14：查询某商品的所有单品

    if re.search(r"(有哪些型号|什么版本|哪几款|有哪些款|所有单品|全部型号)", text):

    return "查询某商品的所有单品"

    return None

    def has_word(self, category, text):

    return any(word in text for word in self.synonyms[category])

    def has_price_range(self, text):

    """检测价格"""

    return re.search(

    r"(\d+[元块]?至\d+[元块]?|\d+-\d+[元块]?|[\d一二三四五六七八九十百千万]+元)",

    text,

    )

    def has_attribute_value(self, text):

    """检测属性值"""

    return re.search(r"\d+[gG][bB]|\d+寸|\d+英寸|\d+毫米", text)

    def has_brand_word(self, text):

    """检测品牌"""

    return any(word in text for word in self.synonyms["品牌"][:5])

    def has_category_word(self, text):

    """检测品类"""

    return any(word in text for word in self.synonyms["品类"][5:])

    基于规则的实体抽取

    分别使用关键词匹配、正则表达式匹配、倒排索引匹配、模糊匹配方式抽取实体。

    src\entity_extractor_rule_base.py：

    import re

    import jieba

    from neo4j import GraphDatabase

    from collections import defaultdict

    from rapidfuzz import fuzz, process

    from config import NEO4J_URI, NEO4J_AUTH

    class EntityExtractorRuleBase:

    """实体抽取-基于规则"""

    def __init__(self):

    with GraphDatabase.driver(uri=NEO4J_URI, auth=NEO4J_AUTH) as driver:

    初始化关键词字典

    self.entity_dict = {

    "品类": [

    record[0]

    for record in driver.execute_query(

    "MATCH (category3:Category3) RETURN category3.name"

    )[0]

    ],

    "品牌": [

    record[0]

    for record in driver.execute_query(

    "MATCH (trademark:Trademark) RETURN trademark.name"

    )[0]

    ],

    "商品": [

    record[0]

    for record in driver.execute_query(

    "MATCH (spu:SPU) RETURN spu.name"

    )[0]

    ],

    "属性": {},

    }

    for record in driver.execute_query(

    "MATCH (attr:Attr) RETURN attr.type, attr.name"

    )[0]:

    价格属性不需要构造实体字典

    if record["attr.type"] == "价格":

    continue

    self.entity_dict["属性"].setdefault(record["attr.type"], []).append(

    record["attr.name"]

    )

    构造关键词倒排索引

    self.spu_index = self.build_inverted_index(self.entity_dict["商品"])

    def __call__(self, text, schema: list) -> dict:

    """实体抽取，更新实体槽位"""

    slots = {}

    使用关键词匹配实体

    for entity_type in schema:

    if entity_type in ["品牌", "品类", "商品"]:

    for value in self.entity_dict[entity_type]:

    if value and value in text:

    slots[entity_type] = [value]

    break

    if "属性" in schema:

    slot_attr_dict = {}

    for attr_type, attr_names in self.entity_dict["属性"].items():

    for attr_name in attr_names:

    if attr_name and str(attr_name) in text:

    slot_attr_dict.setdefault(attr_type, []).append(attr_name)

    break

    slots["属性"] = []

    for attr_type, attr_names in slot_attr_dict.items():

    slots["属性"].extend(attr_names)

    slots["属性"] = list(set(slots["属性"]))

    if "商品属性类型" in schema:

    for attr_type, attr_names in self.entity_dict["属性"].items():

    if attr_type and attr_type in text:

    slots.setdefault("商品属性类型", []).append(attr_type)

    使用倒排索引匹配商品实体

    if "商品" in schema and slots.get("商品") is None:

    res = self.query_inverted_index(text, self.spu_index)

    if res:

    slots["商品"] = [res]

    else:

    使用模糊匹配提取实体

    match = process.extractOne(

    text, self.entity_dict["商品"], scorer=fuzz.ratio, score_cutoff=60

    )

    if match:

    slots["商品"] = [match[0]]

    使用正则表达式匹配价格

    if "价格" in schema:

    pattern = r"(\d+(?:\.\d+)?)\s*(?:元|块|人民币|RMB|￥)"

    matches = re.findall(pattern, text)

    if matches:

    slots["价格"] = matches

    return slots

    def build_inverted_index(self, entity_list):

    """构建关键词倒排索引"""

    inverted_index = defaultdict(list)  # 关键词->实体列表

    for entity_name in entity_list:

    提取实体名称中的关键词

    keywords = jieba.lcut(entity_name)

    for keyword in keywords:

    keyword = keyword.strip().lower()

    if keyword and len(keyword) > 1:

    inverted_index[keyword].append(entity_name)

    return inverted_index

    def query_inverted_index(self, text, inverted_index):

    """查询倒排索引"""

    best_res = None

    candidates = defaultdict(int)  # 候选实体->分数

    提取文本中的关键词

    keywords = jieba.lcut(text)

    for keyword in keywords:

    keyword = keyword.strip().lower()

    for entity_name in inverted_index.get(keyword, []):

    candidates[entity_name] += 1

    if candidates:

    选择出现次数最多的实体作为匹配结果

    if max(candidates.values()) >= 2:

    best_res = max(candidates, key=candidates.get)

    return best_res

    if __name__ == "__main__":

    eerb = EntityExtractorRuleBase()

    res = eerb(

    "联想拯救者Y9000P有哪些配置？",

    ["品牌", "品类", "商品", "商品属性类型", "属性", "价格"],

    )

    print(res)

    整体流程

    src\dialog_process.py：

    import re

    import config

    from neo4j import GraphDatabase

    from config import NEO4J_URI, NEO4J_AUTH

    from intent_recognize_rule_base import IntentRecognizeRuleBase

    from entity_extractor_rule_base import EntityExtractorRuleBase

    from entity_extractor_model_base import EntityExtractorModelBase

    from models_def import IntentClassifyModel, SpellCheckModel, load_params

    class DialogProcess:

    def __init__(self):

    self.intent_recognize_rule_base = IntentRecognizeRuleBase()

    self.intent_classify_model = IntentClassifyModel(

    config.PRE_TRAINED_DIR / "bert-base-chinese", config.INTENT

    )

    self.spell_check_model = SpellCheckModel(

    config.PRE_TRAINED_DIR / "bert-base-chinese"

    )

    self.entity_extractor_rule_base = EntityExtractorRuleBase()

    self.entity_extractor_model_base = EntityExtractorModelBase(

    config.BASE_DIR / "uie_pytorch/checkpoint/model_best"

    )

    load_params(

    self.intent_classify_model, config.MODELS_DIR / "intent_classify.pt"

    )

    load_params(self.spell_check_model, config.MODELS_DIR / "spell_check.pt")

    self.driver = GraphDatabase.driver(NEO4J_URI, auth=NEO4J_AUTH)

    def entity_extract(self, text, schema):

    slots = self.entity_extractor_rule_base(text, schema)

    rest_schema = list(set(schema) - set(slots.keys()))

    if not rest_schema:

    slots.update(self.entity_extractor_model_base(text, rest_schema))

    rest_schema = list(set(schema) - set(slots.keys()))

    if rest_schema:

    return None

    return slots

    def __call__(self, text):

    意图识别-规则

    intent = self.intent_recognize_rule_base(text)

    意图识别-模型

    if not intent:

    intent = self.intent_classify_model.predict(text)

    拼写纠错

    text = self.spell_check_model.predict(text)

    slots = {}

    实体抽取

    match intent:

    case "查询某商品的某个属性的属性值":

    slots = self.entity_extract(text, ["商品", "商品属性类型"])

    if slots:

    cypher = """

    MATCH (spu:SPU)-[:HAVE]->(:SKU)-[:HAVE]->(attr:Attr)

    WHERE spu.name = $spu_name AND attr.name = $attr_name

    RETURN attr.name

    LIMIT 25

    """

    params = {

    "spu_name": slots["商品"][0],

    "attr_type": slots["商品属性类型"][0],

    }

    records = self.driver.execute_query(cypher, params)[0]

    res = [r["attr.name"] for r in records]

    response = f"{slots['商品'][0]}的{slots['商品属性类型'][0]}有\n- {'\n- '.join(res)}"

    return response

    case "查询某商品的所有单品":

    slots = self.entity_extract(text, ["商品"])

    if slots:

    cypher = """

    MATCH (spu:SPU)-[:HAVE]->(sku:SKU)

    WHERE spu.name = $spu_name

    RETURN sku.name

    LIMIT 25

    """

    params = {"spu_name": slots["商品"][0]}

    records = self.driver.execute_query(cypher, params)[0]

    res = [r["sku.name"] for r in records]

    response = f"{slots['商品'][0]}有\n- {'\n- '.join(res)}"

    return response

    case "查询某商品具有某些属性值的单品":

    slots = self.entity_extract(text, ["商品", "属性"])

    if slots:

    cypher = """

    MATCH (spu:SPU)-[:HAVE]->(sku:SKU)-[:HAVE]->(attr:Attr)

    WHERE spu.name = $spu_name AND attr.name IN $attr_list

    RETURN sku.name

    LIMIT 25

    """

    params = {"spu_name": slots["商品"][0], "attr_list": slots["属性"]}

    records = self.driver.execute_query(cypher, params)[0]

    res = [r["sku.name"] for r in records]

    response = f"符合条件的有\n- {'\n- '.join(res)}"

    return response

    case "查询某商品具有某些属性值的单品的价格":

    slots = self.entity_extract(text, ["商品", "属性"])

    if slots:

    cypher = """

    MATCH (spu:SPU)-[:HAVE]->(sku:SKU)-[:HAVE]->(attr:Attr),(sku)-[:HAVE]->(price:Attr{type:"价格"})

    WHERE spu.name = $spu_name AND attr.name IN $attr_list

    RETURN sku.name,price.name

    LIMIT 25

    """

    params = {"spu_name": slots["商品"][0], "attr_list": slots["属性"]}

    records = self.driver.execute_query(cypher, params)[0]

    res = [f"{r['sku.name']} : {r['price.name']}" for r in records]

    response = f"符合条件的有\n- {'\n- '.join(res)}"

    return response

    case "查询某品类某价格区间的单品":

    slots = self.entity_extract(text, ["品类", "价格"])

    if slots:

    slots["价格"] = [

    float(re.sub(r"[^\d.]", "", str(price_str)))

    for price_str in slots["价格"]

    ]

    if len(slots["价格"]) == 1:

    price_range = [slots["价格"][0] 0.8, slots["价格"][0] 1.2]

    else:

    price_range = sorted(slots["价格"][:2])

    cypher = """

    MATCH (category3:Category3)<-[:BELONG]-(spu:SPU)<-[:BELONG]-(sku:SKU)-[:HAVE]->(price:Attr{type:"价格"})

    WHERE category3.name = $category3_name AND price.name >= $left_price AND price.name <= $right_price

    RETURN sku.name

    LIMIT 25

    """

    params = {

    "category3_name": slots["品类"][0],

    "left_price": price_range[0],

    "right_price": price_range[1],

    }

    records = self.driver.execute_query(cypher, params)[0]

    res = [f"{r['sku.name']}" for r in records]

    response = f"价格位于[{price_range[0]},{price_range[1]}]的{slots['品类'][0]}有\n- {'\n- '.join(res)}"

    return response

    case "查询某品类某品牌某价格区间的单品":

    slots = self.entity_extract(text, ["品类", "品牌", "价格"])

    if slots:

    slots["价格"] = [

    float(re.sub(r"[^\d.]", "", str(price_str)))

    for price_str in slots["价格"]

    ]

    if len(slots["价格"]) == 1:

    price_range = [slots["价格"][0] 0.8, slots["价格"][0] 1.2]

    else:

    price_range = sorted(slots["价格"][:2])

    cypher = """

    MATCH (category3:Category3)<-[:BELONG]-(spu:SPU)<-[:BELONG]-(sku:SKU)-[:HAVE]->(price:Attr{type:"价格"}),(spu)-[:BELONG]->(trademark:Trademark)

    WHERE category3.name = $category3_name AND trademark.name = $trademark_name AND price.name >= $left_price AND price.name <= $right_price

    RETURN sku.name

    LIMIT 25

    """

    params = {

    "category3_name": slots["品类"][0],

    "trademark_name": slots["品牌"][0],

    "left_price": price_range[0],

    "right_price": price_range[1],

    }

    records = self.driver.execute_query(cypher, params)[0]

    res = [f"{r['sku.name']}" for r in records]

    response = f"价格位于[{price_range[0]},{price_range[1]}]的{slots['品牌'][0]}{slots['品类'][0]}有\n- {'\n- '.join(res)}"

    return response

    case "查询某品牌所有品类":

    slots = self.entity_extract(text, ["品牌"])

    if slots:

    cypher = """

    MATCH (trademark:Trademark)<-[:BELONG]-(:SPU)-[:BELONG]->(category3:Category3)

    WHERE trademark.name = $trademark_name

    RETURN category3.name

    LIMIT 25

    """

    params = {"trademark_name": slots["品牌"][0]}

    records = self.driver.execute_query(cypher, params)[0]

    res = [f"{r['category3.name']}" for r in records]

    response = f"{slots['品牌'][0]}的品类有\n- {'\n- '.join(res)}"

    return response

    case "查询某品类所有品牌":

    slots = self.entity_extract(text, ["品类"])

    if slots:

    cypher = """

    MATCH (trademark:Trademark)<-[:BELONG]-(:SPU)-[:BELONG]->(category3:Category3)

    WHERE category3.name = $category3_name

    RETURN trademark.name

    LIMIT 25

    """

    params = {"category3_name": slots["品类"][0]}

    records = self.driver.execute_query(cypher, params)[0]

    res = [f"{r['trademark.name']}" for r in records]

    response = f"{slots['品类'][0]}的品牌有\n- {'\n- '.join(res)}"

    return response

    case "查询某品类所有商品":

    slots = self.entity_extract(text, ["品类"])

    if slots:

    cypher = """

    MATCH (spu:SPU)-[:BELONG]->(category3:Category3)

    WHERE category3.name = $category3_name

    RETURN spu.name

    LIMIT 25

    """

    params = {"category3_name": slots["品类"][0]}

    records = self.driver.execute_query(cypher, params)[0]

    res = [f"{r['spu.name']}" for r in records]

    response = f"{slots['品类'][0]}的商品有\n- {'\n- '.join(res)}"

    return response

    case "查询某品类某个属性的所有属性值":

    slots = self.entity_extract(text, ["品类", "商品属性类型"])

    if slots:

    cypher = """

    MATCH (spu:SPU)-[:BELONG]->(category3:Category3)

    WHERE category3.name = $category3_name

    RETURN spu.name

    LIMIT 25

    """

    params = {"category3_name": slots["品类"][0]}

    records = self.driver.execute_query(cypher, params)[0]

    res = [f"{r['spu.name']}" for r in records]

    response = f"{slots['品类'][0]}的商品有\n- {'\n- '.join(res)}"

    return response

    case "查询某品类某品牌的所有商品":

    slots = self.entity_extract(text, ["品类", "品牌"])

    if slots:

    cypher = """

    MATCH (trademark:Trademark)<-[:BELONG]-(spu:SPU)-[:BELONG]->(category3:Category3)

    WHERE category3.name = $category3_name AND trademark.name = $trademark_name

    RETURN spu.name

    LIMIT 25

    """

    params = {

    "category3_name": slots["品类"][0],

    "trademark_name": slots["品牌"][0],

    }

    records = self.driver.execute_query(cypher, params)[0]

    res = [f"{r['spu.name']}" for r in records]

    response = f"{slots['品牌'][0]}的{slots['品类'][0]}的商品有\n- {'\n- '.join(res)}"

    return response

    case "查询某品类具有某些属性值的单品":

    slots = self.entity_extract(text, ["品类", "属性"])

    if slots:

    cypher = """

    MATCH (attr:Attr)<-[:HAVE]-(sku:SKU)-[:BELONG]->(:SPU)-[:BELONG]->(category3:Category3)

    WHERE category3.name = $category3_name AND attr.name IN $attr_list

    RETURN sku.name

    LIMIT 25

    """

    params = {

    "category3_name": slots["品类"][0],

    "attr_list": slots["属性"],

    }

    records = self.driver.execute_query(cypher, params)[0]

    res = [f"{r['sku.name']}" for r in records]

    response = f"符合{slots['属性']}的{slots['品类'][0]}有\n- {'\n- '.join(res)}"

    return response

    case "查询某品类某品牌具有某些属性值的单品":

    slots = self.entity_extract(text, ["品类", "品牌", "属性"])

    if slots:

    cypher = """

    MATCH (attr:Attr)<-[:HAVE]-(sku:SKU)-[:BELONG]->(spu:SPU)-[:BELONG]->(category3:Category3),(spu)-[:BELONG]->(trademark:Trademark)

    WHERE category3.name = $category3_name AND trademark.name = trademark_name AND attr.name IN $attr_list

    RETURN sku.name

    LIMIT 25

    """

    params = {

    "category3_name": slots["品类"][0],

    "trademark_name": slots["品牌"][0],

    "attr_list": slots["属性"],

    }

    records = self.driver.execute_query(cypher, params)[0]

    res = [f"{r['sku.name']}" for r in records]

    response = f"符合{slots['属性']}的{slots['品牌'][0]}{slots['品类'][0]}有\n- {'\n- '.join(res)}"

    return response

    case "查询和某商品某个属性具有相同属性值的其他商品":

    slots = self.entity_extract(text, ["商品", "商品属性类型"])

    if slots:

    cypher = """

    MATCH (spu1:SPU)<-[:BELONG]-(:SKU)-[:HAVE]->(attr:Attr)<-[:HAVE]-(:SKU)-[:BELONG]->(spu2:SPU)

    WHERE spu1.name = $spu_name AND attr.name IN $attr_list

    RETURN spu2.name

    LIMIT 25

    """

    params = {"spu_name": slots["商品"][0], "attr_list": slots["属性"]}

    records = self.driver.execute_query(cypher, params)[0]

    res = [f"{r['spu2.name']}" for r in records]

    response = f"符合{slots['属性']}的同类商品有\n- {'\n- '.join(res)}"

    return response

    return "抱歉，能说的更具体一些吗"

    import uvicorn

    dialog_process = DialogProcess()

    创建 FastAPI 实例

    app = FastAPI()

    挂载静态文件

    app.mount("/static", StaticFiles(directory="templates"), name="static")

    首页

    async def homepage():

    处理用户消息

    @app.post("/api/chat")

    async def handle_message(request: Request):

    data = await request.json()

    user_message = data.get("message", "")

    resp = dialog_process(user_message)

    return JSONResponse({"message": resp})

    if __name__ == "__main__":

    启动服务
